  $fFloatingComplex_$s$cexpm1 ::
    Complex GHC.Types.Double -> Complex GHC.Types.Double
  [HasNoCafRefs, TagSig: <TagProper>, LambdaFormInfo: LFReEntrant 1,
   Arity: 1, Strictness: <1!P(1!P(L),1!P(L))>, CPR: 1(1, 1),
   Inline: (sat-args=1),
   Unfolding: InlineRule (1, False, False)
              (\ (eta['GHC.Types.Many] :: Complex GHC.Types.Double) ->
               case eta of wild { :+ a b ->
               case a of wild1 { GHC.Types.D# x ->
               case b of wild2 { GHC.Types.D# x1 ->
               case GHC.Prim.<##
                      (GHC.Prim.+## (GHC.Prim.*## x x) (GHC.Prim.*## x1 x1))
                      1.0## of lwild {
                 DEFAULT
                 -> let {
                      expx :: GHC.Prim.Double# [] = GHC.Prim.expDouble# x
                    } in
                    $W:+
                      @GHC.Types.Double
                      (GHC.Types.D#
                         (GHC.Prim.-## (GHC.Prim.*## expx (GHC.Prim.cosDouble# x1)) 1.0##))
                      (GHC.Types.D# (GHC.Prim.*## expx (GHC.Prim.sinDouble# x1)))
                 1#
                 -> let {
                      u :: GHC.Prim.Double# [] = GHC.Prim.expm1Double# x
                    } in
                    let {
                      v :: GHC.Prim.Double# []
                      = GHC.Prim.sinDouble# (GHC.Prim./## x1 2.0##)
                    } in
                    let {
                      w :: GHC.Prim.Double# []
                      = GHC.Prim.negateDouble# (GHC.Prim.*## (GHC.Prim.+## v v) v)
                    } in
                    $W:+
                      @GHC.Types.Double
                      (GHC.Types.D# (GHC.Prim.+## (GHC.Prim.+## (GHC.Prim.*## u w) u) w))
                      (GHC.Types.D#
                         (GHC.Prim.*##
                            (GHC.Prim.+## u 1.0##)
                            (GHC.Prim.sinDouble# x1))) } } } })]

  $fFloatingComplex_$s$clog1mexp ::
    Complex GHC.Types.Double -> Complex GHC.Types.Double
  [HasNoCafRefs, LambdaFormInfo: LFReEntrant 1, Arity: 1,
   Strictness: <1!P(1!P(L),1!P(L))>, CPR: 1(1, 1),
   Inline: (sat-args=1),
   Unfolding: InlineRule (1, False, False)
              (\ (eta['GHC.Types.Many] :: Complex GHC.Types.Double) ->
               case eta of wild { :+ x y ->
               case x of wild1 { GHC.Types.D# x1 ->
               case y of wild2 { GHC.Types.D# x2 ->
               let {
                 expx :: GHC.Prim.Double# [] = GHC.Prim.expDouble# x1
               } in
               let {
                 conrep :: GHC.Prim.Double# []
                 = GHC.Prim.negateDouble#
                     (GHC.Prim.*## expx (GHC.Prim.cosDouble# x2))
               } in
               let {
                 conrep1 :: GHC.Prim.Double# []
                 = GHC.Prim.negateDouble#
                     (GHC.Prim.*## expx (GHC.Prim.sinDouble# x2))
               } in
               let {
                 $j :: Complex GHC.Types.Double <join 0> []
                 = let {
                     eta1 :: GHC.Prim.Double# [] = GHC.Prim.+## 1.0## conrep
                   } in
                   let {
                     eta2 :: GHC.Types.Double [] = GHC.Types.D# eta1
                   } in
                   let {
                     eta3 :: GHC.Types.Double [] = GHC.Types.D# conrep1
                   } in
                   let {
                     eta4 :: Complex GHC.Types.Double []
                     = $W:+ @GHC.Types.Double eta2 eta3
                   } in
                   case $fFloatingComplex_$smagnitude
                          eta4 of wild3 { GHC.Types.D# x3 ->
                   case GHC.Prim.logDouble# x3 of wild4 { DEFAULT ->
                   $W:+
                     @GHC.Types.Double
                     (GHC.Types.D# wild4)
                     ($fFloatingComplex_$sphase eta4) } }
               } in
               case GHC.Prim.<## (GHC.Prim.fabsDouble# conrep) 0.5## of lwild {
                 DEFAULT -> $j
                 1#
                 -> case GHC.Prim.<##
                           (GHC.Prim.fabsDouble# conrep1)
                           0.5## of lwild1 {
                      DEFAULT -> $j
                      1#
                      -> let {
                           x3 :: GHC.Prim.Double# []
                           = GHC.Prim.+##
                               (GHC.Prim.+##
                                  (GHC.Prim.+## conrep conrep)
                                  (GHC.Prim.*## conrep conrep))
                               (GHC.Prim.*## conrep1 conrep1)
                         } in
                         case GHC.Prim./##
                                x3
                                (GHC.Prim.+##
                                   1.0##
                                   (GHC.Prim.sqrtDouble#
                                      (GHC.Prim.+## x3 1.0##))) of wild3 { DEFAULT ->
                         case GHC.Prim.log1pDouble# wild3 of wild4 { DEFAULT ->
                         $W:+
                           @GHC.Types.Double
                           (GHC.Types.D# wild4)
                           (GHC.Float.$fRealFloatDouble_$catan2
                              (GHC.Types.D# (GHC.Prim.+## 1.0## conrep))
                              (GHC.Types.D# conrep1)) } } } } } } })]

                 expx :: GHC.Prim.Float# [] = GHC.Prim.expFloat# x1
               } in
               let {
                 conrep :: GHC.Prim.Float# []
                 = GHC.Prim.negateFloat#
                     (GHC.Prim.timesFloat# expx (GHC.Prim.cosFloat# x2))
               } in
               let {
                 conrep1 :: GHC.Prim.Float# []
                 = GHC.Prim.negateFloat#
                     (GHC.Prim.timesFloat# expx (GHC.Prim.sinFloat# x2))
               } in
               let {
                 $j :: Complex GHC.Types.Float <join 0> []
                 = let {
                     eta1 :: GHC.Prim.Float# [] = GHC.Prim.plusFloat# 1.0# conrep
                   } in
                   let {
                     eta2 :: GHC.Types.Float [] = GHC.Types.F# eta1
                   } in
                   let {
                     eta3 :: GHC.Types.Float [] = GHC.Types.F# conrep1
                   } in
                   let {
                     eta4 :: Complex GHC.Types.Float []
                     = $W:+ @GHC.Types.Float eta2 eta3
                   } in
                   case $fFloatingComplex_$smagnitude1
                          eta4 of wild3 { GHC.Types.F# x3 ->
                   case GHC.Prim.logFloat# x3 of wild4 { DEFAULT ->
                   $W:+
                     @GHC.Types.Float
                     (GHC.Types.F# wild4)
                     ($fFloatingComplex_$sphase1 eta4) } }
               } in
               case GHC.Prim.ltFloat# (GHC.Prim.fabsFloat# conrep) 0.5# of lwild {
                 DEFAULT -> $j
                 1#
                 -> case GHC.Prim.ltFloat#
                           (GHC.Prim.fabsFloat# conrep1)
                           0.5# of lwild1 {
                      DEFAULT -> $j
                      1#
                      -> let {
                           x3 :: GHC.Prim.Float# []
                           = GHC.Prim.plusFloat#
                               (GHC.Prim.plusFloat#
                                  (GHC.Prim.plusFloat# conrep conrep)
                                  (GHC.Prim.timesFloat# conrep conrep))
                               (GHC.Prim.timesFloat# conrep1 conrep1)
                         } in
                         case GHC.Prim.divideFloat#
                                x3
                                (GHC.Prim.plusFloat#
                                   1.0#
                                   (GHC.Prim.sqrtFloat#
                                      (GHC.Prim.plusFloat# x3 1.0#))) of wild3 { DEFAULT ->
                         case GHC.Prim.log1pFloat# wild3 of wild4 { DEFAULT ->
                         $W:+
                           @GHC.Types.Float
                           (GHC.Types.F# wild4)
                           (GHC.Float.$fRealFloatFloat_$catan2
                              (GHC.Types.F# (GHC.Prim.plusFloat# 1.0# conrep))
                              (GHC.Types.F# conrep1)) } } } } } } })]

  $fFoldableComplex :: Data.Foldable.Foldable Complex
  DFunId
  [HasNoCafRefs, TagSig: <TagProper>,
   LambdaFormInfo: LFCon[Data.Foldable.C:Foldable], Inline: CONLIKE,
   Unfolding: DFun:.
                @Complex
                (\ @m
                   ($dMonoid['GHC.Types.Many] :: GHC.Base.Monoid m)
                   (ds['GHC.Types.Many] :: Complex m) ->
                 case ds of wild { :+ a1 a2 -> GHC.Base.mappend @m $dMonoid a1 a2 })
                $fFoldableComplex_$cfoldMap
                $fFoldableComplex_$cfoldMap'
                $fFoldableComplex_$cfoldr
                $fFoldableComplex_$cfoldr'
                $fFoldableComplex_$cfoldl
                $fFoldableComplex_$cfoldl'
                $fFoldableComplex_$cfoldr1
                $fFoldableComplex_$cfoldr1
                $fFoldableComplex_$ctoList
                $fFoldableComplex_$cnull
                $fFoldableComplex_$clength
                $fFoldableComplex3
                  `cast`
                (forall (a :: <*>_N).
                 <GHC.Classes.Eq a>_R
                 %<'GHC.Types.Many>_N ->_R <a>_R
                 %<'GHC.Types.Many>_N ->_R <Complex a>_R
                 %<'GHC.Types.Many>_N ->_R Data.Semigroup.Internal.N:Any[0])
                $fFoldableComplex_$cmaximum
                $fFoldableComplex_$cminimum
                $fFoldableComplex2
                  `cast`
                (forall (a :: <*>_N).
                 <GHC.Num.Num a>_R

  $w$s$csqrt ::
    GHC.Prim.Double#
    -> GHC.Prim.Double# -> (# GHC.Prim.Double#, GHC.Prim.Double# #)
  [HasNoCafRefs, TagSig: <TagTuple[TagProper, TagProper]>,
   LambdaFormInfo: LFReEntrant 2, Arity: 2, Strictness: <L><L>,
   Inline: [2],
   Unfolding: (\ (ww['GHC.Types.Many] :: GHC.Prim.Double#)
                 (ww1['GHC.Types.Many] :: GHC.Prim.Double#) ->
               let {
                 fail :: (# #) -> (# GHC.Prim.Double#, GHC.Prim.Double# #)
                   <join 1> [Arity: 1, Strictness: <A>]
                 = \ (ds['GHC.Types.Many] :: (# #))[OneShot] ->
                   case GHC.Prim.<## ww 0.0## of lwild {
                     DEFAULT
                     -> case GHC.Prim.<## ww1 0.0## of lwild1 {
                          DEFAULT
                          -> case $w$smagnitude ww ww1 of ww2 { DEFAULT ->
                             let {
                               x :: GHC.Prim.Double# []
                               = GHC.Prim.sqrtDouble#
                                   (GHC.Prim./## (GHC.Prim.+## ww2 (GHC.Prim.fabsDouble# ww)) 2.0##)
                             } in
                             case GHC.Prim./##
                                    (GHC.Prim.fabsDouble# ww1)
                                    (GHC.Prim.+## x x) of wild2 { DEFAULT ->
                             (# x, wild2 #) } }
                          1#
                          -> case $w$smagnitude ww ww1 of ww2 { DEFAULT ->
                             let {
                               x :: GHC.Prim.Double# []
                               = GHC.Prim.sqrtDouble#
                                   (GHC.Prim./## (GHC.Prim.+## ww2 (GHC.Prim.fabsDouble# ww)) 2.0##)
                             } in
                             case GHC.Prim./##
                                    (GHC.Prim.fabsDouble# ww1)
                                    (GHC.Prim.+## x x) of wild2 { DEFAULT ->
                             (# x, GHC.Prim.negateDouble# wild2 #) } } }
                     1#
                     -> case GHC.Prim.<## ww1 0.0## of lwild1 {
                          DEFAULT
                          -> case $w$smagnitude ww ww1 of ww2 { DEFAULT ->
                             let {
                               x :: GHC.Prim.Double# []
                               = GHC.Prim.sqrtDouble#
                                   (GHC.Prim./## (GHC.Prim.+## ww2 (GHC.Prim.fabsDouble# ww)) 2.0##)
                             } in
                             case GHC.Prim./##
                                    (GHC.Prim.fabsDouble# ww1)
                                    (GHC.Prim.+## x x) of wild2 { DEFAULT ->
                             (# wild2, x #) } }
                          1#
                          -> case $w$smagnitude ww ww1 of ww2 { DEFAULT ->
                             let {
                               x :: GHC.Prim.Double# []
                               = GHC.Prim.sqrtDouble#
                                   (GHC.Prim./## (GHC.Prim.+## ww2 (GHC.Prim.fabsDouble# ww)) 2.0##)
                             } in
                             case GHC.Prim./##
                                    (GHC.Prim.fabsDouble# ww1)
                                    (GHC.Prim.+## x x) of wild2 { DEFAULT ->
                             (# wild2, GHC.Prim.negateDouble# x #) } } } }
               } in
               case GHC.Prim.==## ww 0.0## of lwild {
                 DEFAULT -> fail GHC.Prim.(##)
                 1#

                      DEFAULT -> fail GHC.Prim.(##) 1# -> (# 0.0##, 0.0## #) } })]

                                    (GHC.Prim.plusFloat# x x) of wild2 { DEFAULT ->
                             (# x, wild2 #) } }
                          1#
                          -> case $w$smagnitude1 ww ww1 of ww2 { DEFAULT ->
                             let {
                               x :: GHC.Prim.Float# []
                               = GHC.Prim.sqrtFloat#
                                   (GHC.Prim.divideFloat#
                                      (GHC.Prim.plusFloat# ww2 (GHC.Prim.fabsFloat# ww))
                                      2.0#)
                             } in
                             case GHC.Prim.divideFloat#
                                    (GHC.Prim.fabsFloat# ww1)
                                    (GHC.Prim.plusFloat# x x) of wild2 { DEFAULT ->
                             (# x, GHC.Prim.negateFloat# wild2 #) } } }
                     1#
                     -> case GHC.Prim.ltFloat# ww1 0.0# of lwild1 {
                          DEFAULT
                          -> case $w$smagnitude1 ww ww1 of ww2 { DEFAULT ->
                             let {
                               x :: GHC.Prim.Float# []
                               = GHC.Prim.sqrtFloat#
                                   (GHC.Prim.divideFloat#
                                      (GHC.Prim.plusFloat# ww2 (GHC.Prim.fabsFloat# ww))
                                      2.0#)
                             } in
                             case GHC.Prim.divideFloat#
                                    (GHC.Prim.fabsFloat# ww1)
                                    (GHC.Prim.plusFloat# x x) of wild2 { DEFAULT ->
                             (# wild2, x #) } }
                          1#
                          -> case $w$smagnitude1 ww ww1 of ww2 { DEFAULT ->
                             let {
                               x :: GHC.Prim.Float# []
                               = GHC.Prim.sqrtFloat#
                                   (GHC.Prim.divideFloat#
                                      (GHC.Prim.plusFloat# ww2 (GHC.Prim.fabsFloat# ww))
                                      2.0#)
                             } in
                             case GHC.Prim.divideFloat#
                                    (GHC.Prim.fabsFloat# ww1)
                                    (GHC.Prim.plusFloat# x x) of wild2 { DEFAULT ->
                             (# wild2, GHC.Prim.negateFloat# x #) } } } }
               } in
               case GHC.Prim.eqFloat# ww 0.0# of lwild {
                 DEFAULT -> fail GHC.Prim.(##)
                 1#
                 -> case GHC.Prim.eqFloat# ww1 0.0# of lwild1 {
                      DEFAULT -> fail GHC.Prim.(##) 1# -> (# 0.0#, 0.0# #) } })]

"realToFrac/a->Complex Double" forall @a
                                      ($dReal['GHC.Types.Many] :: GHC.Real.Real a)
                                      ($dFractional['GHC.Types.Many] :: GHC.Real.Fractional
                                                                          (Complex
                                                                             GHC.Types.Double)).
  GHC.Real.realToFrac @a
                      @(Complex GHC.Types.Double)
                      $dReal
                      $dFractional
  = \ (x['GHC.Types.Many] :: a) ->
    $W:+
      @GHC.Types.Double
      (GHC.Real.realToFrac
         @a
         @GHC.Types.Double
         $dReal
         GHC.Float.$fFractionalDouble
         x)
      (GHC.Types.D# 0.0##)
"realToFrac/a->Complex Float" forall @a
                                     ($dReal['GHC.Types.Many] :: GHC.Real.Real a)
                                     ($dFractional['GHC.Types.Many] :: GHC.Real.Fractional
                                                                         (Complex GHC.Types.Float)).
  GHC.Real.realToFrac @a
                      @(Complex GHC.Types.Float)
                      $dReal
                      $dFractional
  = \ (x['GHC.Types.Many] :: a) ->
    $W:+
      @GHC.Types.Float
      (GHC.Real.realToFrac
         @a
         @GHC.Types.Float
         $dReal
         GHC.Float.$fFractionalFloat
         x)
      (GHC.Types.F# 0.0#)

                      -- | The function 'polar' takes a complex number and
-- returns a (magnitude, phase) pair in canonical form:
-- the magnitude is nonnegative, and the phase in the range @(-'pi', 'pi']@;
-- if the magnitude is zero, then so is the phase.
                    identifiers:
                      {libraries/base/Data/Complex.hs:104:20-24}

                       identifiers:],
          Complex -> [text:
                        -- | Complex numbers are an algebraic type.
--
-- For a complex number @z@, @'abs' z@ is a number with the magnitude of @z@,
-- but oriented in the positive real direction, whereas @'signum' z@
-- has the phase of @z@, but unit magnitude.
--
-- The 'Foldable' and 'Traversable' instances traverse the real part first.
--
-- Note that `Complex`'s instances inherit the deficiencies from the type
-- parameter's. For example, @Complex Float@'s 'Ord' instance has similar
-- problems to `Float`'s.
                      identifiers:
                        {libraries/base/Data/Complex.hs:54:32-34}
                        GHC.Num.abs

                        {libraries/base/Data/Complex.hs:61:49-51}
                        GHC.Classes.Ord
                        {libraries/base/Data/Complex.hs:62:17-21}
                        GHC.Types.Float],
